SoScale1Dragger(3)

Paquete: coin

Licencias: BSD-3-Clause

SoScale1Dragger(3) Coin SoScale1Dragger(3)

NOMBRE

SoScale1Dragger - La clase SoScale1Dragger proporciona un mecanismo para que el usuario final escale en una dimensión.

SINOPSIS

#include <Inventor/draggers/SoScale1Dragger.h>

Hereda de SoDragger.

Fields:

  SoSFVec3f      scaleFactor

Detailed Description

This class defines a 1-axis scale dragger. It is composed of a cube, a line and the scaling gizmo (a small cube).

If the user picks the scaling gizmo and moves the mouse around, the dragger will scale up or down. The dragger is a 1-axis dragger, which means that the scaling will only be applied along one axis (the X-axis by default).

The scaling is done by rotating the dragger around its center and by changing the scaling factor.

Constructor & Destructor Documentation

This class does not define its own constructor or destructor. It inherits the default constructor and destructor from SoDragger.

Member Data Documentation

SoSFVec3f SoScale1Dragger::scaleFactor

Continuously updated to contain the current vector of scaling along the X, Y and Z axes.

For the SoScale1Dragger, only the X component is used, the Y and Z components will always be equal to 1 (i.e. no scaling).
